Annual Report 2025 年報
CORPORATE PROFILE
公司簡介
China Modern Dairy Holdings Ltd. (the “Company” or “Modern Dairy”, stock
code: 1117.HK, collectively with its subsidiaries referred to as the “Group”) is
a leading dairy cows farming operator and raw milk producer in the Chinese
Mainland. We invest in and operate 47 farming companies in the Chinese
Mainland, with 53 modern dairy farms, spanning over 14 provinces, autonomou
regions and municipalities. We raise a herd of over 450,000 dairy cows with
an annualized milk production exceeding 3.33 million tons. The Company
pioneered the “integrated planting, breeding, and processing, zero-distance
2-hour” integrated production model and was the first enterprise in the Chinese
Mainland to adopt such model and obtain SGS certification. Modern Dairy’s
products have been awarded gold medals for twelve consecutive years at the
Monde Selection, which is renowned as the “Nobel Prize” of the food industry.
In recent years, our business segments have also expanded to include forage
grass, digital intelligence platforms, and dairy cows breeding as well.


中國現代牧業控股有限公司（ 「本公司」或「現代牧業」
                         ，股份代號：1117.HK），連同
其附屬公司合稱「本集團」）是國內乳牛畜牧營運商及原料奶生產商的翹楚。我們
在國內投資及營運47個牧場公司，擁有53個現代化牧場，覆蓋14個省份、自治
區及直轄市。飼養逾45萬頭乳牛，年化產奶量達333萬噸以上。本公司首創「種
養加一體化、零距離2小時」是國內首家採用一體化生產模式並獲得SGS認證的
企業。現代牧業的產品連續十二年榮獲世界食品品質評鑑大會金獎，該大會被
譽為食品業的「諾貝爾獎」。近年，我們的業務板塊更擴展至飼草料、數智平台
及乳牛種業等。

of Animal Husbandry, Sharing Responsibilities for a New Journey”.    各界嘉賓雲集，共同回顧集團二十年深耕奶業的
Guests from all sectors joined us in reviewing our 20-year journey   奮鬥之路，見證從行業開創者到領軍者的輝煌跨
in the dairy industry, witnessing our evolution from a pioneer to    越，重溫成長中的每刻感動與榮光。已退休創始
a leader, and reliving the moments of emotion and glory along        人鄧九強先生及高麗娜女士齊齊回來向來賓及團
the way. Retired founders Mr. Deng Jiuqiang and Ms. Gao Lina         隊致辭，感謝每位員工對集團的辛勤付出，員工
returned to headquarters together and addressed the audience,        的堅守，每顆初心、匠心、創新力成就了集團今
thanking every employee for their dedication – emphasizing that      日的輝煌。眾合作夥伴、股東與各界精英對本集
their original aspirations, craftsmanship, and innovative spirit     團的輝煌成就及在乳業上的卓越貢獻予以高度肯
built today’s success. Partners, shareholders, and industry elites   定。慶典在悠揚詩歌中圓滿而歸，員工上下欣喜

REMUNERATION COMMITTEE                                                                  薪酬委員會
The chairman of the Remuneration Committee is Mr. Li Shengli                            薪酬委員會主席為李勝利先生，其他成員為張平
and other members are Mr. Zhang Ping and Mr. Chow Ming Sang.                            先生及周明笙先生。薪酬委員會由獨立非執行董

executive Directors of the Company.

The primary functions of the Remuneration Committee are to                              薪酬委員會之基本職能乃就本集團全體董事及高
make recommendations to the Board on the Company’s policy                               級管理層之本公司薪酬政策及結構向董事會提供
and structure for the remuneration for all the Directors and senior                     建議，就所有執行董事、非執行董事及高級管理
management of the Group, and make recommendations of the                                層之薪酬組合向董事會提供建議。
remuneration packages for all the executive Directors, non-
executive Directors and the senior management to the Board.

T h e C o m p a n y’s e m o l u m e n t p o l i c y i s t o e n s u r e t h a t t h e   本公司之薪酬政策旨在確保向僱員（包括本集團執
remuneration offered to employees including executive Directors                         行董事及高級管理層）提供之薪酬乃基於其技能、
and senior management of the Group is based on the skills,                              知識、職責及參與本公司及本集團事務而釐定，

and the Group’s affairs, and to uphold a fair and competitive                           照本公司之業績及盈利狀況、行業薪酬水平以及
remuneration assessment mechanism. The remuneration                                     當前市場狀況而釐定。獨立非執行董事之薪酬政
packages are also determined by reference to the Company’s                              策（主要包括董事袍金）須參照市場標準每年進行
performance and profitability, remuneration level in the industry                       評估。個別董事及高級管理層不會參與其各自薪
and the prevailing market conditions. The emolument policy for                          酬之釐定。薪酬委員會已獲提供足夠資源履行其
independent non-executive Directors, mainly comprising Directors’                       職責。
fees, is subject to annual assessment with reference to the market
standard. Individual Directors and senior management would not
be involved in deciding their own remuneration. The Remuneration
Committee has been provided with sufficient resources to
discharge its duties.

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL                                            風險管理及內部監控

of the risk management and internal control system of the Group.        系統之有效性。檢討目標主要在於識別風險、保
The review aims primarily to identify risks, protect the Group's        障本集團資產、預防損失、確保本公司財務報告
assets, prevent losses, ensure the accuracy of the Company's            準確無誤及遵守適用法規。檢討範圍由審核委員

The scope of the review will be assessed by the Audit Committee         所有重要監控範圍，包括財務監控、運作監控、
first, and then recommendations will be made to the Board. The          合規監控及風險管理(包括環境、社會及管治)職
review covers all material controls, including financial, operational   能。該檢討亦考慮本公司會計、財務及內部審計
and compliance controls and risk management functions (including        部門之人力資源是否充裕以及是否有足夠具備所
environmental, social and governance functions). Such review also       需資格及經驗之員工、員工培訓計劃及預算。根
considers the adequacy of resources, qualifications and experience      據已批准之監控範圍及程序，將風險管理及內部
of the staff of the Company in relation to its accounting, finance      監控檢討報告傳送予審核委員會成員及董事會，
and internal audit functions, and their training programmes             以便董事會確認本集團在風險管理及內部監控系
and budget. Risk management and internal control review                 統的適當性及有效性。
reports are circulated to the Audit Committee members and the
Board in accordance with the approved monitoring scope and
procedures, so that the Board can confirm the appropriateness and

systems.

During the year, following discussion with and reporting by the         年內，董事會經與審核委員會探討及匯報後，確
Audit Committee, the Board confirmed that the Group has fulfilled       認本集團已履行其在風險管理及內部監控系統方
its responsibilities regarding risk management and internal control     面的責任，並已遵守企業管治守則的守則條文第
systems and it has also has complied with Code Provision D.2            D.2條，建立適當及有效的風險管理及內部監控系

The Group adopts a risk management system which manages                 本集團採納的風險管理系統管理與其業務及營運
the risk associated with its business and operations. The system        相關的風險。該系統包括以下階段：
comprises the following phases:

•    Identification: Identify ownership of risks, business objectives   •   識別：識別風險歸屬、業務目標及可能影響
     and risks that could affect the achievement of objectives.             目標實現的風險。

•    Evaluation: Analyze the likelihood and impact of risks and         •   評估：分析風險的可能性及影響，並據此評
     evaluate the risk portfolio accordingly.                               估風險組合。

•    Management: Consider the risk responses, ensure effective          •   管理：考慮風險回應措施，確保與董事會有
     communication to the Board and on-going monitoring of the              效溝通並持續監控剩餘風險。
     residual risks.

 PRINCIPAL RISKS AND UNCERTAINTIES                                        主要風險及不確定因素
 The Group adopts a comprehensive risk management framework.              本集團採用全面風險管理框架。風險管理政策及
 Risk management policies and procedures are regularly reviewed           程序獲定期檢討並更新以應對乳品質量、食品安
 and updated to react changes in dairy product quality, food              全、生產、動物疾病、環境污染、市場開發及財
 safety, production, animal disease, environment pollution,               務風險變動。審核委員會檢討風險相關政策及仔
 market developments and financial risks. The Audit Committee             細檢查管理層是否已履行其職責，設立對監控及
 reviews risk-related policies and scrutinizes that management has        控制本集團業務活動、外部風險及監管環境變動
 performed its duty to have effective risk management and internal        產生的風險而言屬必要的風險管理及內部監控制
 control systems necessary for monitoring and controlling risks           度，並向董事會就上述情況作出報告。
 arising from the Group’s business activities, changing external risks
 and regulatory environment, and report to the Board on the above.

 integrated operation that based on the strategic plan in order to        密切檢討及監控短長期策略並致力為股東帶來最

 closely review and monitor its short and long-term strategies and

 Operational Risks                                                        營運風險
 Operational risk is concerned with possible losses caused by failure     營運風險有關內部程序失效、人員、制度或外部
 of internal processes, people, system or external events, such           事項造成的可能損失，此風險透過建立健全的內
 risk is mitigated by and controlled by establishing sound internal       部監控、明確責任範圍、適當的職責分離及有效
 control, setting out clear lines of responsibility, proper segregation   的內部報告予以降低及控制。管理層定期檢討本
 of duties and effective internal reporting. Management regularly         集團營運以確保本集團虧損（不論是財務或其他方
 reviews the Group’s operations to ensure that the Group’s risk of        面）、欺詐所導致、錯誤、疏忽或其他營運及合規
 losses, whether financial or otherwise, resulting from fraud, errors,    事宜的風險得到充分管理。
 omissions and other operational and compliance matters, are
 adequately managed.

 exposure. Furthermore, with aim to tackle the risk of interest rate      可考慮在必要時訂立利率掉期合約或其他對沖策
 fluctuation, the management regularly reviews and assesses the           略。
 Group’s exposure to interest rate movement and may consider

   Islands.

         For each business combination, the Group can elect to                 就每項業務合併而言，本集團可選擇

         Group are presented on the face of the consolidated                   集團業績中的非控制權益在綜合損益
         statement of profit or loss and the consolidated                      表及綜合損益及其他全面收益表中列

                 between the contractual and expected amounts.                         期現金差額的現值計量。

                       effective interest rate;                                            利率；

                 The maximum period considered when estimating                         估計預期信貸虧損時所考慮的最

                 which the Group is exposed to credit risk.                            最長合約期間。

                 bases:                                                                個基準計量：

                       12 months); and                                                     部分預期信貸虧損；及

                 The Group considers a financial asset to be in                        本集團認為金融資產在下列情況

                 The Group considers a financial instrument to                         本集團認為，當金融工具的信貸

                 investment grade.                                                     信貸風險較低。

                 ECLs are remeasured at each reporting date to                         預期信貸虧損於各報告日期進行
                 reflect changes in the financial instrument’s credit                  重新計量以反映金融工具自初步
                 risk since initial recognition. Any change in the                     確認以來的信貸風險變動。預期
                 ECL amount is recognised as an impairment gain                        信貸虧損金額的任何變動均於損
                 or loss in profit or loss. The Group recognises an                    益確認為減值收益或虧損。本集
                 impairment gain or loss for all financial instruments                 團就所有金融工具確認減值收益
                 with a corresponding adjustment to their carrying                     或虧損，並通過虧損撥備賬對其
                 amount through a loss allowance account, except                       賬面值作出相應調整，惟按公平
                 for investments in non-equity securities that are                     值計入其他全面收入（可撥回）計
                 measured at FVTOCI (recycling), for which the loss                    量的非股權證券投資除外，該等

                          consider otherwise;                                                          款或墊款之條款；

                 of impairment in profit or loss in the period in
                 which the recovery occurs.

                    The recoverable amount of an asset or CGU is                             資產或現金產生單位的可收回金
                    the greater of its value in use and its fair value                       額是其使用價值及其公平值減出
                    less costs of disposal. Value in use is based on the                     售成本兩者中的較高者。使用價
                    estimated future cash flows, discounted to their                         值基於估計未來現金流量，按可
                    present value using a pre-tax discount rate that                         以反映當時市場對貨幣時間值及
                    reflects current market assessments of the time                          資產或現金產生單位特定風險的
                    value of money and the risks specific to the asset                       評估的稅前貼現率，貼現至其現
                    or CGU.                                                                  值。

                    An impairment loss is recognised if the carrying                         當資產或現金產生單位的賬面值
                    amount of an asset or CGU exceeds its                                    高於其可收回金額時，減值虧損
                    recoverable amount.                                                      乃獲確認。

                    Impairment losses are recognised in profit or loss.                      減值虧損於損益中確認，會首先
                    They are allocated first to reduce the carrying                          分配至減少已分配至該現金產生
                    amount of any goodwill allocated to the CGU, and                         單位的任何商譽的賬面值，然後
                    then to reduce the carrying amounts of the other                         按比例減少現金產生單位內其他
                    assets in the CGU on a pro rata basis.                                   資產的賬面值。

                    An impairment loss in respect of goodwill is not                         有關商譽的減值虧損不會撥回。
                    reversed. For other assets, an impairment loss                           就其他資產而言，減值虧損僅在
                    is reversed only to the extent that the resulting                        所得賬面值不高於假設並無確認
                    carrying amount does not exceed the carrying                             減值虧損時原應釐定的已扣除折
                    amount that would have been determined, net of                           舊或攤銷的賬面值的範圍內才予
                    depreciation or amortisation, if no impairment loss                      以撥回。
                    had been recognised.

   (v)   Income tax                                                        (v)   所得稅

            item reported in the financial statements, are identified          目的金額，乃根據向本集團各業務線
            from the financial information provided regularly to the           分配資源及評估其業績表現以及地理
            Group’s most senior executive management for the                   位置而定期向本集團最高行政管理層

            performance of, the Group’s various lines of business
            and geographical locations.

            Individually material operating segments are not                   個別重大經營分部不會就財務報告目
            aggregated for financial reporting purposes unless the             的而合併，除非相關分部具有類似的

            or provide the services, and the nature of the regulatory          別重大經營分部可合併。
            environment. Operating segments which are not
            individually material may be aggregated if they share a
            majority of these criteria.

    Assumptions were used in the value-in-use calculation of each            各現金產生單位或現金產生單位組別的使用
    CGU or group of CGUs. The following describes each key                   價值計算採用了假設。以下描述了管理層為
    assumptions the management has based on in preparing the                 進行減值測試而編製現金流量預測時所依據
    cash flow projections for the purpose of impairment test:                的各項關鍵假設：

    •       The estimated future market price of raw milk, the               •    原料奶的估計未來市場價格、每個泌
            estimated average daily milk yield at each lactation                  乳週期的估計平均日產奶量及奶牛養
            cycle and estimated feed costs per kg of raw milk for                 殖每公斤原料奶的估計飼料成本；及

            for feeds or services – such assumptions are based                    假設基於現金產生單位或現金產生單
            on the CGU’s or group of CGUs’ past performance                       位組別過去的業績及管理層對市場發

            group of CGUs.                                                        位組別相關的特定風險。

            account the industry growth rate, past experience and                 單位或現金產生單位組別的行業增長

          the estimated fair value on the date of transfer.

                                                                                本集團的中國僱員為中國政府營辦的國家管

    by the government of the PRC. The employees of the PRC
                                                                                求每月向國家管理退休福利計劃作出供款以
    subsidiaries of the company are required to contribute in
                                                                                撥付有關福利。本集團於國家管理退休福利
    accordance with employee pension insurance requirements

    on monthly basis to the state-managed retirement benefit
                                                                                供款。

    schemes.

   (f)   Capital risk management                                                     (f)   資本風險管理
         The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in                          本集團的資金管理旨在確保本集團旗
         the Group will be able to continue as a going concern                             下實體的持續經營能力，同時透過優
         while maximising the return to stakeholders through                               化債務及股本結餘為利益相關者謀求
         the optimisation of the debt and equity balance. The                              最大回報。本集團的整體策略較去年
         Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged from prior                             保持不變。
         year.

         The capital structure of the Group consists of net                                 本集團的資本架構由債務淨額（主要包
         debt, which mainly includes bank borrowings, other                                 括銀行借款、其他借款、長期債券、
         borrowings, long term bonds, lease liabilities and                                 租賃負債及短期融資券（分別披露於附
         short term debenture as disclosed in Notes 28, 29,                                 註28、29、30、31及32），扣除銀行結
         30, 31 and 32 respectively, net of bank balances and                               餘及現金）及本公司權益股東應佔權益
         cash and equity attributable to equity shareholders of                            （包括已發行股本、股份溢價及儲備）
         the Company, comprising issued share capital, share                                組成。
         premium and reserves.

         T h e G r o u p’s m a n a g e m e n t r e v i e w s t h e c a p i t a l           本集團的管理層定期檢討資本架構。
         structure on a regular basis. As part of the review, the                          作為檢討的一環，管理層會考慮資本
         management considers the cost of capital and the                                  成本及各類資本涉及的風險。本集團
         risks associated with each class of capital. The Group                            透過發行新股份及發行新債務或贖回
         will balance its overall capital structure through the                            現有債務等方式保持整體資本架構的
         issues of new shares and the issue of new debt or the                             平衡。
         redemption of existing debt.

      period on a recurring basis, categorised into the three-            於報告期末按經常性基準計量的金融工具公
      level fair value hierarchy as defined in IFRS 13, Fair value        平值。公平值計量所歸入的級別參照估值方
      measurement. The level into which a fair value measurement          法所用輸入數據的可觀察性及重要性釐定如

                              fail to meet Level 1, and not using                  察輸入數據，且不使用重

                              Unobservable inputs are inputs for                   的公平值。不可觀察輸入

                              unobservable inputs                                  據計量的公平值

      The Group has a team headed by the finance manager                  本集團擁有一支由財務經理領導的團隊，負
      performing valuations for the financial instruments, including      責對金融工具進行估值，包括非上市權益證
      unlisted equity securities and conversion option embedded           券及嵌入可轉換票據中的轉換選擇權，這些
      in convertible notes which are categorised into Level 3 of          金融工具被歸類為公平值等級中的第三級。
      the fair value hierarchy. The team reports directly to the          該團隊直接向首席財務官及審核委員會匯報
      chief financial officer and the audit committee. A valuation        工作。團隊於每個中期及年度報告日期編製
      report with analysis of changes in fair value measurement is        估值報告，分析公平值計量的變化，並由首

      officer. Discussion of the valuation process and results with       告日期一致。

      CFCCS            Garman-Kohlhagen model and Discounted             N/A                                     N/A
      CFCCS              cash flow                                       不適用                                     不適用
                       Garman-Kohlhagen模型及貼現現金流量

                       Since it is fixed-to-fixed cross currency swap,
                          future cash flows are estimated based
                          on the contract. Different cash flows in
                          different currencies are discounted via
                          the corresponding curves, and netted
                          into the same currency as at the valuation
                          date.
                       由於是固定至固定的跨貨幣掉期，未來現金
                          流量乃根據合約估計。不同貨幣的不同
                          現金流量通過相應的曲線貼現，並在估
                          值日以相同的貨幣淨額計算。

      and the Offer”) as detailed in the relevant announcement or        函中詳述的可能強制有條件現金要約（    「收購
      circulars disclose on and after 30 October 2025.                   事項及要約」   ）。

      As the Acquisition and the Offer has not been completed            由於收購事項及要約於本報告日期尚未完
      as at the date of this report, the transaction has no impact       成，因此該交易對本集團截至2025年12月
      on the consolidated financial position or performance of the       31日止年度的綜合財務狀況或業績並無影
      Group for the year ended 31 December 2025. Due to the              響。由於收購事項及要約仍在進行中，目前
      ongoing nature of the Acquisition and the Offer, it is not yet     仍無法對收購事項對本集團未來綜合財務報

 44. POSSIBLE IMPACT OF AMENDMENTS,                                     44. 於截至2025年12月31日止年度
     NEW STANDARDS AND                                                      已頒佈但尚未生效的修訂、新訂
     INTERPRETATIONS ISSUED BUT NOT YET                                     準則及詮釋的可能影響（續）
     EFFECTIVE FOR THE YEAR ENDED 31
     DECEMBER 2025 (Continued)
      The Group is in the process of making an assessment of              本集團正對於初始應用期間預期存在的此等
      what the impact of these developments is expected to be in          改進的影響作出評估。迄今之結論為，除以
      the period of initial application. So far it has concluded that     下情況外，採納該等改進不大可能對綜合財
      the adoption of them is unlikely to have a significant impact       務報表產生重大影響：
      on the consolidated financial statements except for the
      following:

      IFRS 18, Presentation and disclosure in financial                   國際財務報告準則第18號財務報表呈列
      statements                                                          及披露
      IFRS 18 will replace IAS 1 Presentation of financial statements     國際財務報告準則第18號將取代國際會計准
      and aims to improve the transparency and comparability of           則第1號財務報表呈列 ，旨在提高有關實體
      information about an entity’s financial statements. IFRS 18 is      財務報表資料的透明度及可比性。國際財務
      effective for annual reporting periods beginning on or after 1      報告準則第18號於2027年1月1日或之後開始
      January 2027 and is to be applied retrospectively.                  的年度報告期間生效，並將追溯適用。

      Among other changes, under IFRS 18, entities are required           除其他變化外，根據國際財務報告準則第18
      to classify all income and expenses into five categories            號，實體必須於損益表中將所有收入及開支
      in the statement of profit or loss, namely the operating,           分為五類，即經營、投資、融資、已終止經
      investing, financing, discontinued operations and income            營業務及所得稅類別。實體亦需在財務報表
      tax categories. Entities are also required to provide specific      的單一附註中提供有關管理層定義的績效指
      disclosures about management-defined performance                    標的具體披露內容。
      measures in a single note in the financial statements.

      The Group does not plan to early adopt IFRS 18 and is still in      本集團不打算提前採納國際財務報告準則第
      the process of assessing the impact of the adoption.                18號，目前仍在評估採用該準則的影響。
